{"step_index":929,"source":"MODEL","type":"PLANNER_RESPONSE","status":"DONE","created_at":"2026-04-28T12:15:48Z","content":"There are three effective ways to handle this, ranging from fully manual to fully automatic. Since this is a **Leave Tracker**, the most logical approach is to use the data the system already has:\n\n### 1. Automatic Detection (Recommended)\nThe system checks its own database to see if the General Manager has an **Approved Leave Request** for the current date.\n\n*   **How it works**: When a request reaches the `PENDING_GM` state, the backend runs a query: *\"Is there an approved leave record for the User with role 'GM' where today's date falls between their startDate and endDate?\"*\n*   **Pros**: Zero manual effort; completely data-driven.\n*   **Cons**: If the GM is away for a meeting (not official leave), the system won't know.\n\n### 2. Manual \"Away\" Toggle\nAdd a setting in the GM's profile or an Admin dashboard called **\"Out of Office / Delegate Approval\"**.\n\n*   **How it works**: When the GM knows they will be unreachable, they flip a switch in the app. This sets a `is_away` flag in the `User` table.\n<truncated 1519 bytes>"}
